# Third-party notices

Clairix is free software under the GNU General Public License, version 3 (see
[LICENSE](LICENSE)). It is built with other people's open-source work, each
used under its own licence.

**In the app**, Settings → About → Third-party licences lists every Android
library in that build, the code compiled into them and every Rust crate in the
encryption library, each with its licence in full. The build writes that list
itself (`thirdPartyNotices` in `android/app/build.gradle.kts`): the AboutLibraries
Gradle plugin reads the licences the Android libraries publish, and the licence
texts, and the native code no library's metadata names, are in
`android/app/notices`.

**This file** is generated by cargo-about 0.9.2 from `comms-crypto/about.hbs`
(see `comms-crypto/about.toml`). `.github/workflows/android.yml` regenerates it
and fails when it differs from what is committed, so edit the template, not
this file.

## Android libraries

The libraries the app declares in `android/app/build.gradle.kts`, and the
licences they are published under. The libraries these depend on in turn are
in the app's list.

| Library | Licence |
|---|---|
| AndroidX (core, lifecycle, activity, appcompat, biometric, sqlite) and Jetpack Compose, by the Android Open Source Project | Apache-2.0 |
| CameraX, by the Android Open Source Project | Apache-2.0; the libyuv code in it BSD-3-Clause |
| Kotlin standard library and kotlinx.coroutines, by JetBrains | Apache-2.0 |
| Google Play services location | Android Software Development Kit License (developer.android.com/studio/terms) |
| osmdroid 6.1.20 | Apache-2.0 |
| OkHttp 4.12.0 and Okio, by Square | Apache-2.0 |
| SQLCipher for Android 4.17.0, by Zetetic | SQLCipher Community Edition License (BSD-style) |
| JNA 5.19.1 (Java Native Access) | LGPL-2.1-or-later or Apache-2.0, at the user's choice |
| ZXing 3.5.4 and ZXing Android Embedded 4.3.0 | Apache-2.0 |
| UnifiedPush connector 3.3.5 | Apache-2.0 |
| Stream WebRTC Android 1.3.10 | Apache-2.0; the WebRTC library in it, see below |

## Compiled into those libraries

Two of those libraries carry native code built from other projects, which no
library's metadata names:

- **Stream WebRTC Android** (`libjingle_peerconnection_so.so`) is WebRTC
  (BSD-3-Clause, with WebRTC's patent grant), compiled with Abseil (Apache-2.0),
  BoringSSL, dav1d (BSD-2-Clause), libaom (BSD-2-Clause, with the AOMedia patent
  licence), libevent (BSD-3-Clause), libsrtp (BSD-3-Clause), libvpx
  (BSD-3-Clause, with its patent grant), libyuv (BSD-3-Clause), Opus
  (BSD-3-Clause) and Protocol Buffers (BSD-3-Clause).
- **SQLCipher for Android** (`libsqlcipher.so`) is SQLCipher, compiled with
  SQLite and LibTomCrypt, both in the public domain.

Their licence texts are in `android/app/notices/licences` and in the app's
list, except BoringSSL's and libevent's, which the app's list links to.

## Fonts and data

- The Archivo and JetBrains Mono fonts: SIL Open Font License 1.1
  (`licenses/OFL-1.1-Archivo.txt`, `licenses/OFL-1.1-JetBrains-Mono.txt`).
- Map data: © OpenStreetMap contributors, available under the Open Database
  Licence (ODbL) at openstreetmap.org/copyright. The maps credit it on screen.
  Their tiles come from OpenStreetMap's tile server unless the signed tile
  setting (`relays/tiles.json`) names another; an online place search goes to
  OpenStreetMap's Nominatim.
- The stalkerware list: converted from Stalkerware Indicators of Compromise by
  Julien Voisin and Tek for Echap (github.com/AssoEchap/stalkerware-indicators),
  under the Creative Commons Attribution 4.0 licence.

## Rust crates in the encryption library

`libclairix_comms_crypto.so` (`comms-crypto/`) compiles in these crates, for
the Android targets the app is built for. Each licence below is the text of
the crate's own licence file, with its copyright lines; where a crate ships no
file for the licence it is used under, it is that licence's standard text.

- Apache License 2.0 (Apache-2.0): 333 crates
- MIT License (MIT): 89 crates
- ISC License (ISC): 21 crates
- Unicode License v3 (Unicode-3.0): 19 crates
- BSD 3-Clause "New" or "Revised" License (BSD-3-Clause): 12 crates
- Mozilla Public License 2.0 (MPL-2.0): 10 crates
- zlib License (Zlib): 3 crates
- BSD 2-Clause "Simplified" License (BSD-2-Clause): 1 crate
- Creative Commons Zero v1.0 Universal (CC0-1.0): 1 crate
- The Unlicense (Unlicense): 1 crate

#ifndef OPENSSL_HEADER_EC_ECP_NISTZ384_H
#define OPENSSL_HEADER_EC_ECP_NISTZ384_H

#include "../../limbs/limbs.h"

#define P384_LIMBS (384u / LIMB_BITS)

typedef struct {
  Limb X[P384_LIMBS];
  Limb Y[P384_LIMBS];
  Limb Z[P384_LIMBS];
} P384_POINT;

typedef struct {
  Limb X[P384_LIMBS];
  Limb Y[P384_LIMBS];
} P384_POINT_AFFINE;


#endif // OPENSSL_HEADER_EC_ECP_NISTZ384_H

// CONNECTION WITH THE USE OR PERFORMANCE OF THIS SOFTWARE.

//! EdDSA Signatures.

use super::ops::ELEM_LEN;
use crate::digest;

pub mod signing;
pub mod verification;

/// The length of an Ed25519 public key.
pub const ED25519_PUBLIC_KEY_LEN: usize = ELEM_LEN;

pub fn eddsa_digest(signature_r: &[u8], public_key: &[u8], msg: &[u8]) -> digest::Digest {
    let mut ctx = digest::Context::new(&digest::SHA512);
    ctx.update(signature_r);
    ctx.update(public_key);
    ctx.update(msg);
    ctx.finish()
}

````

use crate::limb::Limb;

#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
#[repr(transparent)]
pub struct N0([Limb; 2]);

impl N0 {
    #[cfg(feature = "alloc")]
    pub(super) const LIMBS_USED: usize = 64 / crate::limb::LIMB_BITS;

    #[inline]
    pub const fn precalculated(n0: u64) -> Self {
        #[cfg(target_pointer_width = "64")]
        {
            Self([n0, 0])
        }

        #[cfg(target_pointer_width = "32")]
        {
            Self([n0 as Limb, (n0 >> crate::limb::LIMB_BITS) as Limb])
        }
    }
}

use crate::error::{KeyRejected, Unspecified};

impl From<untrusted::EndOfInput> for Unspecified {
    fn from(source: untrusted::EndOfInput) -> Self {
        super::erase(source)
    }
}

impl From<core::array::TryFromSliceError> for Unspecified {
    fn from(source: core::array::TryFromSliceError) -> Self {
        super::erase(source)
    }
}

impl From<KeyRejected> for Unspecified {
    fn from(source: KeyRejected) -> Self {
        super::erase(source)
    }
}

    sha2_32::{block_data_order_32, State32, SHA256_BLOCK_LEN},
    sha2_64::{block_data_order_64, State64, SHA512_BLOCK_LEN},
};

pub(super) const CHAINING_WORDS: usize = 8;

#[cfg(any(
    all(target_arch = "aarch64", target_endian = "little"),
    all(target_arch = "arm", target_endian = "little"),
    target_arch = "x86_64"
))]
#[macro_use]
mod ffi;

pub(super) mod fallback;
mod sha2_32;
mod sha2_64;

````
